They had become so close and then the moment when Lena called her in to get the Lawyers together and brought Catco, why not, she would be able to spend more time with Kara, although she missed her boss Sam was nice, a little flakey and seemed to be off but her daughter Ruby was indeed a breath of fresh air and a welcome addition to the office. She remembered the return of Mike, and the conversation with Lena after she returned following the lead issue, she was happy to have her back but there was something off, Jess found that Mike was Kara’s boyfriend and she was momentarily confused by this new information and Lena had said that Kara and her were best friends, nothing more. She could see the sadness behind her bosses eyes when she had said that, but Jess, ever the professional, had not said anything. After all, she held more secrets than the DEO, yes she had finally worked that out too, there was a level of anonymity to being an assistant, other than Lena and Kara most people paid her no mind.

 

Jess had not realised until it was way too late that Sam was the new villain Reign. Lena had been spending more time in her personal lab and working to try and help Sam, she had witnessed the destruction of the friendship between the Super and the Luthor, that hack James Olsen’s interference increasing the damage ten fold, even Lena and Kara seemed to be struggling. She figured that it was a separation of church and state, a new dimension that they were navigating, she didn't think for a moment that Lena didn't know Kara’s secret. For all her observational skills and intelligence she missed what was in front of her face the whole time. Lena Luthor, genius, did not know that Kara Danvers was Supergirl, The phrase Love is blind, was never more accurate when it came to Lena and Kara. Jess had always assumed that like her Lena just said nothing about the duality, to avoid the unpleasantries and well because as much as Kara was adorably clumsy and terrible at keeping her secrets, however smooth she thought she was. Jess assumed that like her Lena just pretended not to know, not to see the cracks in her disguise. It appeared on reflection that Lena actually had no idea that the two were one and the same, she had never seen Lena so broken, or had been more withdrawn than when her brother had tried to kill her or when Jack died. The difference being that the cause of the pain was the person that normally pulled her out of it.

 

*********** LCorp 1 year ago***************

 

“Jess can you come in here please”

 

Jess replied and headed into the office, something had been off about Lena, she wasn't sure what but she knew it had something to do with Kara, she had not sparkled as much, the visits seemed tense and unpleasant, it was a few days before the pulitzer party and she was confused as to why Lena wasn't more happy that Kara had won such a prestigious award, sure it was a exposay on Lena’s brother but still, she had not been upset at the initial article, she had gushed about it. However since her brother fell she was different colder. She had once said that her brother died a long time ago, maybe the emotions were no longer able to sit in their boxes.

 

“How can I help Miss Luthor?”

 

“Jess this contract is in need of certification, however it is top secret and I need it to be done and returned today, I know that you have work to do but you are the only one I can trust with this.”

 

Jess smiled and took the packet, she would head immediately and have it back before the end of the day she knew that Lena didn't ask for something to be turned around so fast unless it was important she smiled and headed out. It wasn't until she sat with the notary that she realised it was the sale of Catco. She had been shocked but it wasn't her place to question her boss or her decisions, maybe she had finally given up hope that there would ever be more between her and Kara, she would make sure that she did more to take care of Lena, after all she had taken a back seat to the blonde, not that she minded at the time.

 

***********LCorp 6 months ago ************

 

The last six months had been hell, Jess sat outside of Lena’s office, she could see the destruction the mess that was happening all around her, she had been actively removing Kara, she had been going up against Supergirl, she was treating them both with disdain. Clearly Lena had finally realised that they were the same, she was hurting badly and not handling it well. Lena was distancing herself from everyone, even her relationship with her boss was being affected, the CEO was shutting off from everything and she missed Lena so very much. Jess missed the woman that she once was, the woman that had become her boss, her hero, the woman she admired more than anyone else not to mention that she was slowly being replaced by a computer. An artificial intelligence, Lena was alone and pulling away from all human interactions, she barely saw her now and was no longer enjoying working for the CEO, it was breaking the assistants heart. She’d had several offers made to her and finally made up her mind, if she couldn't get through to the woman that she had looked up to once and admired for so long, then it was time to go. She stood and knocked on the door, Lena had called her in.

 

“What is the problem Jess?”

 

“Can I speak candidly Miss Luthor?”

 

Jess had moved toward the desk and stood tall looking at Lena, she looked tired, her jaw was set, there was more than a little stress and anger on her face. She knew that there was no longer a good time to do or ask for anything from Lena any more. Jess was always able to read when she could do and say things a little more than assistant, normally after a visit from Kara, and the closeness and openness that Lena carried for an hour or so after a visit, that was gone. Lena was nowhere to be seen, she had met the other Luthors, and the person on the other side of the desk was a Luthor and that thought only spurred her on even more. Lena was not like the rest of her family and Jess was not going to leave without trying to save the woman that had saved so many others before, she had nothing to lose that she had not lost already.

 

“Jess I am incredibly busy right now, if this isn't work related I just don't have time, if you need time off schedule it with Hope”

 

“Well it is work related, not that it will matter much past today, I came to work for you not for safety and security, heaven knows I have had more near misses and injuries than a cage fighter since working here, it wasn't for the money or prestige either, I came here to work for Lena Luthor, the woman that I admired for her drive, ambition, ideals, determination and intelligence. For over four years I have been a loyal employee, I have worked hard and loved being a part of what you have achieved. I even stood by and watched while you moved that two faced backstabbing troll of a woman Tessmacher over from CatCo to be your lab assistant and held my tongue when she tried to all but take my job out from under me. Did I whine and complain, no I did not. I stayed and did my job as always to the best of my capabilities and gave 100% to whatever it was that you had instructed of me,but no more, now you are replacing me with a goddamn computer, removing all people from your life and turning into a goddamn robot yourself. I never thought I would see the day that I came to work for a woman so devoid of passion and light that the most humanity I see in a day is interacting with a discombobulate voice of an AI, with the personality of a sadistic megalomaniac, with the power to remove free will and the ability to lie. You are hiding from your own pain by attempting to make us all automatons, it's been tried before, it didn't work then and it isn't going to work now or make you happy. Don't worry about calling for security to have me removed, my desk is already packed up. I will not need an escort as I will gladly leave the building, but you owe me at least the opportunity to finish what I have to say.”

 

Jess tilted her head and clenched her jaw and stood her ground watching Lena’s hand move away from the hidden button, clearly she was either shocked at her soon to be former assistants words or going to let her commit career suicide, after all she requested it. Jess took a deep breath and gathered her thoughts then prepared to continue.

 

“I get it ok, you have been hurt, lied to and you are lashing out, and this time had no one to call, because the person that hurt you was Kara, the one person on this planet that you love more than anything. You may not see me as a friend but I saw you as one Miss Luthor because I cared about you, I have kept caring and trying for the last six months, but I can not do it any more. Honestly it’s hurting me so much to see the woman I have looked up to and admired for so long become exactly the person that you fought so hard for others not to see you as. Being the smartest person in the room, in most every room that you are ever in, you have to have had realisation that you missed the truth for so very long. While it must have hurt you beyond any measure of words, you have to know very well why you missed it? It is simply because you love her and that reason alone, that you missed seeing the truth for so long. You think she betrayed you, that maybe so but I have watched you love her with such a passion and force that is unmatched by anyone else, except by the way she has loved you in return for the past four years, in both her costumes.

 

Yes she lied, but she was here, she was always here even when you didn't want her to be just as she promised, she protected you in every way possible, even risked her own secret identity to do it putting your life before anything else. I have waited for you to pull yourself together, but all you have done is tare yourself further apart and I can not watch this any more. As Arthur Schopenhauer said “All truth passes through three stages. First, it is ridiculed. Second, it is violently opposed. Third, it is accepted as being self-evident.” I have waited for you to get to stage three, but instead you're burying yourself so deep within your projects and artificial “people” that you are not getting there. Be angry, scream, shout, do whatever you need, but at the end of it all there is only the simple and irrefutable fact. Pushing her away, pretending you dont care, that you don't love, that you're not really a human being. That is certainly not helping, you are just hurting yourself and I will not stay to watch the most amazing woman I have ever had the pleasure to know vanished into insanity. One truth is self-evident to me, Kara being Supergirl means that even before you knew you loved her, both sides of her loved you. It's sad to see that you would rather lose them both than love her fully, you Lena Luthor the woman who won't live in fear is a coward. I would give you notice, but well with hope, you don't need me”

 

********* Now********

 

Jess smiled thinking back to that moment, she had not even made it out of the building before security had caught up with her and dragged her back to Lena’s office. Lena had cleared her schedule, and talked to Jess like they used to. It had taken a while but Kara was back, their relationship was on the mend and by the looks of things stronger than ever, Jess and Lena had become closer. The A.I. Hope, well she was banished to a small shelf in a volt. Jess just couldn't believe that of all things an emotional outburst was what finally tipped Lena over the edge, seeing Jess of all people crack had made her stop and take a moment to realise how far she had fallen, and she had gone to Kara the day after. It felt as if the veil had been lifted, even if Jess had to have that horendus meeting happen and she had to fill out so much paperwork that it seemed ridiculous since the woman had known for years and yet still not breathed a word to anyone. Once again Jess had a front row seat and she was sure that not only Lena but Kara had woken up to what was happening between them, she was a little more tentative about going into the office now. She kept expecting there to be something more than just a snuggle on the couch or a tight embrace, but so far there had been nothing. She wondered if there was still a block in the way, was there something keeping them from taking that final step to being what everyone could see they were supposed to be.

 

****** 4 days ago******

 

Jess went into the office and handed Lena over the tickets for her flight, she was on the red eye flight meaning that she needed to head off soon, but she insisted on getting everything up and ready for her absence. Lena was the head, and the heart of LCorp but she was needed elsewhere.

 

“Lena, you need to pack up here are your tickets, I will make sure everything runs smoothly over the next three days dont worry.”

 

“Ah yes, that is something else, you are having the next three days off, so you will not be here to take care of things, there is a temp handling the calls, and you are turning your phone off and heading out, in oh in about five minutes.”

 

Lena handed her an envelope and she tilted her head looking as Lena waved it in front of her. She took the envelope and looked up a little confused.

 

“Jess its your birthday, and between getting LCorp, and its CEO back on track, you have had no time off in the past six months, you are going to a all expenses paid spa for the next three days, you will have no work, no pressure and no I repeat no interruptions from me or LCorp. I will take your phone if I have to.”

 

“I um, wow, that is too much Lena that, I will be available, what if you need me? If they try and pull something? I can't just go off to a spa and leave you alone to face that shark tank.”

 

“Jess I have your personal mobile, and if I am surrounded in chum and they are circling I will call, but other than an actual life or death situation you, you are on vacation ”

 

********* Now**********

 

She opened her bag and picked out her work phone, she had not had it on for the last three days, Lena had her personal number if it was an emergency but she had insisted that she took three days off. She turned it on and placed it on the seat, there was no point in looking until the frantic beeping finished. She sat back and looked out the window, dusk was in full swing and soon it would be dark. There was a small amount of traffic she hoped that it wouldn't delay her, she wanted to meet Lena. Jess picked up the phone, there was nothing from Lena in three days, there want a call, text, or email, there were a number of very annoyed emails from investors and board members on Lena’s emails, she figured that Lena must have caused more turmoil than expected, she opened one and scanned it, Lena had blown them off. That wasn't right, in minutes she had gone through looking for anything, Lena had not booked in on the flight, at the hotel, on the return flight, the private Jet was still being repaired, the assistant had not heard from her, there were no news items, Lena had not been spotted in days. Jess didn't panic often but this was so far out of character for Lena. She called the head of security, Lena had not been in the office, her card had not been used in any LCorp facilities or subsidiaries since she left the office, after Jess herself left. She called the building, there was nothing on the monitors for the private elevator to the penthouse. Lena never made it home, there were no claims to have her, no ransoms. She scrolled through the phone and found the number she was looking for dialing and waiting patiently for an answer.

 

“Jess hey, Is everything ok? What time is Lena back, I was um, I was going to call you so I could meet her at the airport, but she said that you were on leave, that she had insisted, I can't say I disagree with her desire to make you take care of yourself too. I have been really excited all day, listening out for her return but I am guessing that she is coming back late, she has not been replying to messages, I was starting to think that there was something wrong, but then she said before she went that she would probably be a little busy and distracted. I almost headed out just so I could check on her, I am not used to not hearing from her, I know that it isn't the same but I started to feel like she was gone again, my sisters girlfriend suggested that I um, oh well that is um nothing that is important now, I just I realised I was overreacting. Um, sorry what was it you wanted?”

 

“Lena is missing Kara, I am in the car on the way to pick her up, she didn't make her flight, she has been gone for three days. There is no one claiming they have her. Kara… Kara are you listening…. Kara”

4. The search Begins

Summary for the Chapter:

Jess and Kara start the search for Lena, and Lena battles her attraction to Kara.

 

“Brainy, deep search, any movement of Kryptonite, Vasquez work with Jess, find the car that Lena left in, Johnson, active threats against Luthor, known associates of Lillian and Lex, anyone looking to make her pay for anything. Kara you should check the office and the penthouse, see if there is anything amiss there, see if you can find anything, stay on coms, check everything out, don't just run in without making sure it is safe. I’ll take point. Everyone back in thirty for an update, time is of the essence we are chasing our tails, go, go, go.”

Notes for the Chapter:

I hope you liked the last chapter as much as I did, I cant help but love that we as a fandom have created a body of work rich and diverse over a Asian American Woman who was in one scene - I Love Jess, she in my mind is the ever loving and caring assistant, and the one to give those fools in love a swift kick in the pants. More to come.....

Jess felt the car stop abruptly and then the door opened, none other than National Cities hero sliding into the car. Jess didn't know if she should laugh or cry at the prompt response from the hero. She looked up and could see the worry on her face and she settled to look at her, she could see that there was a level of anxiety radiating off the blonde . She wondered how Kara would have been able to go three days without hearing from Lena and not be panicked, although she suggested that she had been. She wondered how long this would have gone on if she had not decided to go to the airport, after all it was now Friday, God Lena could have been missing for six days before anyone noticed. She felt sick and her heart rate spiked what was happening to Lena.

 

“Hey Jess, no panicking ok, you need to tell me what you know so we can set about finding her, she isn't in the city that I know”

 

“I left for my vacation, she left the building about ten minutes after me and got into a town car, she didn't arrive at the airport, she didn't check in, there is no record of her arriving checking in or coming back, so she must have been taken between getting in the car and arriving at the airport, security is getting the footage, but we need to track the car to see where she ended up. Why do you think that she isn't in the city? Her Jet is still being fixed, and well that means that she must have been taken by car”

 

Kara listened out again stretching her hearing as far as she could, she took a breath. She didn't want to frighten Jess any more than she already was after all she could hear the pounding of her heart. The heart she had not heard in three days was Lena’s and that was something that was playing on her mind right now. She looked at Jess clearly she was heading down a dark road and she refused to believe that Lena was no longer with them, she knew that she couldn't hear Alex when she was hidden in a bunker of lead, if Lena was somewhere protected she wouldn't be able to hear her, she was definitely missing though this additional information was complete clarity in the fact that Lena Luthor was indeed missing.

 

“I can hear her heartbeat for about 120 miles, if I really concentrate, but it vanished four days ago I assumed when she moved out of range on the plane, but that doesn't mean that she is hurt or dead Jess, there are limits to my powers and there are ways to hide her heartbeat from me….. Um, we need to go to the DEO and get things started. How do you feel about Flying Jess?”

 

“F-flying with you??”

 

**********Penthouse********

 

Lena woke up in the bathtub, the darkened bathroom seemed to cause no issue for her eyes, she was able to see as clearly as when it was light, that was new. She had noticed that she could see clearer during the day, her eyes had been irritated by the contacts she had initially been wearing, like they were trying to focus on something both near and far. 5. The truth in plain sight

Summary for the Chapter:

Lena finds the truth of her situation and tried to make a move to help herself, realising that it is not possible she needs to call in some reinforcements.

Lena sneered at him, she beat her childhood, she beat Lillian, she beat Lex she would beat this. There was no way she could get to the lab like this, but how would she get what she needed.

Notes for the Chapter:

A little DR L Luthor Scientist genius, beautiful human, and a little Jess subterfuge.

No real hate towards Stephanie Mayer, I love the world she created, and I hope there will be a complete compliment of Midnight sun!! although sparkling vamps are a little odd :)

Lena finally opened the program link to the server, it wasn't the LCorp one that she needed but the portal to the Luthor corp server that was embedded in the background. It was like an archive system that she created to ensure that the information was available but not to the general staffers. Using the portal was better than slipping into the LCorp servers using the backdoor, it was untraceable and wouldn't tip anyone off to her presence. Performing a basic search for Hanson, Lena found the projects logged under his name and Luthor-corp ID number, there was nothing there that had any significance, nothing that she had not seen before, all the information on the regeneration project was limited only referencing its termination. “Many have heard it, but nobody has ever seen it. It will not speak back until spoken to. What is it?”

She typed in Echo and moved on to the next, five of the most boring science riddles ever, nothing that would cause too much trouble really, most people could have googled the answer to them, she guessed that it was smart if it was something hard then people would think that it was something worth hiding, something silly and childish would maybe be overlooked. What is the physical significance of order (q) when calculating the Information entropy?

 

She typed in the formula, S = (1/1-q) log (Σ p^q), then the schrodinger equation, Einsteins, and finally they were over the last test was chess, a game against the computer, the game was already in play by her assessment there was twelve moves until the computer won, without a number of very strategic plays, however nothing that Lena couldn't accomplish in her sleep. “Now we have a sustainable viral load to deliver and have been able to complete a full cellular transformation with complete reconstruction of the genetic code, although the subjects are completely stable, specimens begin to degrade after a period of time unable to sustain the transformation. Subjects are unable to consume food or water as it has 0 effect on their sustainability and longevity, however they do have a base need for blood, it appears that is the key element in sustaining the transformation keeping subjects fully functional. As a result we have had to keep the active number of test subjects low to sustain project viability. The drain on our blood supplies has been substantial, having to subsidise with blood from workers, even I am having to donate, however we have found that 1 in five of the subjects have an additional advantage, it appears to come in the form of mental or emotional manipulations. No transmogrifications, then we may be using a fictional label for the viral changes, they seem to have no flight or turning into a bat, not that I would truly expect such a ridiculous ability. Advanced strength, speed, hearing and vision have presented, there also appears to be enhanced healing too. Subjects appear to be able to sustain upto 60% organ and skin damage yet still recover. Decapitation, more specifically separation of the cranium from the body or severing the vertebrae anywhere along the cervical, thoracic, lumbar, sacrum spine and down to coccyx, will result in termination of the test subject. However this defect can be utilized as a failsafe or self destruct if the subject is overtly hostile and cannot be controlled or is compromised. A small capsule of compressed explosive has been inserted into the cervical spine of all subjects between the atlas and axis vertebrae, should the need arise all subjects can be terminated immediately via remote access.

 

Exposure to sunlight also causes a uniquely incendiary reaction depending on the amount of UV light or exposure to natural sunlight, if using artificial UV light in a concentrated beam, subjects skin ignites in flame and continues to burn having similar reaction as a human would with a laser scalpel. Full body exposure to either artificial UV or natural sun to subjects cause the entire body to ignite into flame and burn, as did the first initial successful test subject. Some specimens present other weaknesses depending on their own personal or religious beliefs. When informed that they are vampires the more susceptible of mind, easily swayed to common delusional mass hysteria. Crosses, garlic and holy water only works on the particularly religious, whereas the other items are fatal. We need to increase the level of intelligence in the participants to see if the trends follow if we can increase the additional powers. Jess had worked for LCorp for Lena for 4 years, but she knew about the Lex years, there was nothing if not a lot of gossip in the secretarial pool, there were rumours that Lex wasn't human long before he went completely insane, his appearance more like the walking dead, and there was an area that he spent most of his time, there were all kinds of absurd theories about armies of undead, that he was messing with zombies, and aliens, the theories never ended, however looking at the request from Lena one think popped into her mind. Jess had a guilty pleasure, all things vampire, she loved it from the old hammer horrors, british films so corny but beautiful, then there was Bram Stoker and all the people that followed, there was always something so beautiful in the dynamics, the struggles, they created a worlds where epitome of mankind’s struggles could be played out with veracity, the good the bad. It made more sense that this was a test, a test of her, someone out there could be testing her to see if they could get something different something more. Was it really Lena? Was someone playing on her emotional attachment to her boss, her friend? The elevator was full of boxes and Jess moved into the elevator, she had never betrayed Lena before, the only time she had gone against her in any way was when she threatened to leave, and if this meant her being in danger too so be it, if it was Lena in there she would forgive her an indiscretion something minor. At least she hoped so, she rode the elevator up to the penthouse and chewed on her lip nervously, she hoped that this wouldn't put Lena in any danger, she hoped that she was doing the right thing, as the doors opened she saw a small flash of a body moving.

 

Lena had been able to smell Jess the moment that she disobeyed her direct order not to get in the elevator, she could smell the scent wafting up through the shaft, she could hear her heart beating the blood passing around her system, she could feel her teeth elongating and her saliva pooling in her mouth. She could hardly focus on anything it was like ever instinct was now focused on feeding like, all she needed to do was rip through the doors and jump down the shaft to get to her quicker, she was pacing trying to stop herself from just heading to her, to her meal, Oh god all she could think of was sinking her teeth into her neck and draining her dry, the scent was not as delectable as Kara but it was so good at this point she was so hungry she wasn't sure that she would be able to hold back. Jess was her friend, she kept reciting it over and over and then the door opened and Jess’s scent assaulted her olfactory senses, she growled and moved back as quick as she could, away as far away as she could.

 

“Jess I told you to stay away, what you are doing, close the door, get away from here, God Jess please I don't want to hurt you.”

 

“I….I just needed to know you….. You are alright, P-please let me help you, Lena please, I have the supplies, I just need to know you are not being hurt.”

 

Kara heard Lena’s voice, she was confused, there was someone with Lena’s voice but Lena was not there, there was no heartbeat, there was nothing to tell her that Lena was in that apartment. Jess had spoken back and she was scared, why would Lena scare her, but that couldn't be Lena there was no heart rate in there at all other than Jess’s that was beating frantically, was it a hologram? Even aliens have a heartbeat? An AI? She didn't have much more time to contemplate this thought as there was sudden movement and a scream, she flew through the window and headed towards the scream Jess was backing away from a figure, as the glass shattered the figure spun and launched at Kara with a speed that actually caught her off guard and tackled her to the floor, the strength the speed, she looked into the face of what was and was not Lena.

6. Is IT Lena?

Summary for the Chapter:

Kara and Jess argue over the situation while Lena is turned into a hunger grazed primal being.

Kara watched as Lena spoke, there was a moment before she opened her mouth that she watched numerous thoughts spin through her mind, she knew the look was Lena’s contemplation look, the one that she first had on her face when she mentioned her mother being part of CADMUS. The way that her mind would filter through a million things at once, normally followed by the stoic mask that she often wore when making a decisive action or making a statement. Kara was expecting something like that now, but instead Lena still seemed unsure, taking just the smallest of breaths before she spoke. There was a hesitancy, something that she knew Lena felt, but not something that she ever showed to people, well other than Kara that is.

Everything was moving so fast, one second Kara had heard Jess scream, the next moment she was crashing through the window of Lena’s home. The sounds of snarling and smashing reverberated throughout the upscale apartment, only accompanied by the shrill of Jess’s screams. Kara wrestled the imposter back and forth neither one gaining the upper hand, flipping over and over destroying everything in their way as they rampaged through the penthouse deadlocked. Kara silently prayed to Rao when all this was over, Lena would forgive her for the destruction and mess the hero was making in her home. The girl of steel found herself once again on her back, the snapping jaw and sharp fangs of the imposter attempting to gain purchase on her neck. Kara was stronger and faster, but her opponent seemed to somehow be using hero’s own strength against her. Obviously, her adversary was more inherently cunning than she had initially assumed, she moved just enough to rotate their position to counterbalance. Forcing her hands into a locked position holding off the attacker long enough to get a good look at her foe.

The pale alabaster skin a shade lighter, the hair just as dark contrasting, silky smooth with a slight wave, the perfect eyebrows sculpted and beautiful, the eyes, pupils blown with a small ring of red before the classic mix of jade, emerald, gold and spots of hazel. That perfectly angular and sharp jawline that looked could cut marble, yet somehow soft and smooth. The bow of the scarlet painted lips, then the teeth, sharp and terrifying. It was Lena, no… No… it could not be. Not the woman she had known for so many years and inevitably fallen in love with. Kara couldn’t deny that this impostor very closely resembled the green-eyed beauty, but it was not her Lena. Above her was a fake, a fraud, snarling, snapping, drooling above her, growling at Kara like some wild beast emanating from deep in her chest. A primal desire was present, was it to kill? to hurt her. Everything was unclear, she flipped their positions slamming the imposter into the rear wall, flinching for a split second as she felt the force of the body hit concrete wall. “JESS GET OUT OF HERE””

Jess couldn't believe this was happening right now, the one person who has always believed in Lena was now doubting what was right in front of her face. Jess thought about how most myths and vampire stories always started with blood lust, the instinctual need, hunger and thirst for blood. No matter what movie or story is told, there is always an initial period where a new vampire needs to feed and regain their humanity.

Lena had been able to contact her to work out what she needed and get help, that means Lena had been able to stave off hunger for a while at least, Jess had no idea when she had been turned or had woken up. Kara had cleared the penthouse earlier not finding the CEO, she must have been able to resist the need to feed then. Only five minutes ago Lena had the strength to get close enough to push Jess away and try to save her, however her attention was hyper focused when Kara arrived, going after the Kryptonian with pure unrestrained focus. There were myths about the connection of love and attraction between the undead and humans. Dracula and Mina Harker, best friend of Lucy Westenra who eventually became a vampire, both vampires became obsessed with Mina. Alaric and Caroline, and of course, Edward and Bella, ok so sparkling vampires that walk in the sun were ridiculous and a total crock of shite, really not the best reference but it still showed a connection.

“I’m not leaving not while you two are fighting, God Damn it Kara she’s just hungry and she is clearly under the influence of something. What happened when you were exposed to Red K, I saw what you became when you were not yourself. You need to stop and think damn it! Kara, you know her, look at her, it's Lena.”

“Shoot, Jess, I can't keep you safe, whatever this is has no control, get out of here I can't protect you”

Jess needed to get Kara to really look at Lena, the Kryptonian loved Lena with everything she had, there nothing was more obvious except for the fact that Lena was just as much in love with the blonde. Really who would be more irresistible than ball of sunshine Kara Danvers. Lena needed to take the edge off her base primal need, she needed to feed to ease her frenzy and reclaim her mind. The last thing Jess needed was to be protected in this situation, it was Lena that was in danger and on the edge of becoming out of control. It was the one thing that she feared of becoming, nothing but a mindless monster, a Luthor. If Lena gives in to her desires and could even puncture Kara’s impenetrable skin and drink from the Kryptonian, if she drained her, Lena would never forgive herself. Jess needed a plan fast, to help Lena regain control, Kara was not seeing this was Lena, how could she not? Maybe something was stopping her seeing it? “Kara, hold her still, I have an idea.”

“Shut it Danvers, just hold her still. You’ll both thank me later”

Kara looked at Jess like the woman was out of her mind, she could see her moving from the elevator with something in her hand. What in Rao’s name was Jess thinking, wait up, is she holding a blood bag? What the heck, could this woman not understand the danger she was in. There appeared to be no stopping the determined woman, she was clearly invested in this theory whatever it was. Kara would like to have been told about it before the fragile human approached whatever this thing that she was fighting. She thought back to some of the aliens that she had faced, Parasite had drained people and objects of energy, but parasite had a heartbeat, then the snake like alien that ate human hearts, that too had a heartbeat. Hellgrammites needed the chemicals in DDT to feed on to survive, again they had a heartbeat. What kind of Alien would need blood and have no heartbeat? Maybe it was a meta human? Kara remembered there were several meta’s that had strange gifts and unusual needs. The only things without a heartbeat were holograms, robots and dead people. This was none of those things she was sure of that, however that didn't give her any idea about what it actually was, but Jess seemed to, even if she was clinging to the misguided hope that whatever this was it was their Lena. Pinning down her adversary she watched its fangs snap viciously up at her, waiting as Jess moved forward pushing the blood bag into the fangs and mouth.

Lena hadn’t been able to concentrate on anything since the moment Kara burst through the window, everything was on autopilot just trying to get to what she needed, the blood coursing through Kara’s body. The more the blonde fought to struggle with Lena the faster the blood had pumped, pushing the fluid through her veins, the contractions of her beating heart pounding in her ears. Saliva pooled in her mouth the delectable scent filling the penthouse and throat burned like it was on fire. Lena moved with single minded determination and focus of sinking her teeth into her sweet smelling Kryptonian and draining her dry, she was only mildly aware of Jess’s presence to her. The woman’s scent had not been of interest to her since the moment that Kara entered the penthouse, she heard screaming but there was little comprehension of what was being said. Then she found herself on her back snapping up at Kara, her scent so close she was surrounded in it, her throat burned hotter and more vibrant than before, radiating from the centre of her throat down through her chest. Lena didn't register the hand moving or the bag it was holding until her teeth connected with the plastic, the initial taste was chemical and then all of a sudden there was fluid seeping into her mouth and for the first time since she woke up, she could feel the viscous fluid soothing the burn. To finally be able to stand in National City and know that Reign was gone, that it was just her and that the City had survived, that Kara had survived. She needed to go to her mothers place and confront that too, but her fear was not something that was going to stop her help, Lena, not this time.

 

“Lex.. you know that he always has the last word, well this one was possibly his best yet.”

 

“What did he do Lena? I thought he was dead? How has he done anything? You know what, never mind, it’s Lex Luthor, nothing that he managed to do even from the grave is going to surprise me, I mean it isn't like he didn't have the resources to pull almost anything off, I am shocked he didn't find a way to cheat death itself.”

 

“He did, but he didn’t use it on himself, instead he turned me into a monster for his amusement, even though his final message showed no remorse. He said and I quote, Ironically if you are watching this it means that I am dead and you were visited by Otis, you see you called me a monster, you said that all Luthor’s were and that you didn't want to be one anymore. So I had a thought, how ironic and beautiful worthy of a true Luthor. You see dear sister, if you will not willingly act like a Luthor, then I shall force you to be the monster you fear all Luthor's to be. Instinctual, predatory, taking from others with no consequence considered. The irony is, if I didn't die of natural causes then you killed me, proving that you are already a Luthor, you will be the biggest monster of us all.”

 

Sam really didn't have a clue what Lex was suggesting but whatever it was the words themselves were chilling, Lena had not denied that she was the one to take Lex out of this world and Sam knew that the only one that would ever be able to get close enough to him to do it would be Lena, he let his guard down around her always underestimating her, she was the superior Luthor in every way. However there was nothing that he could do to make Lena a monster, sure she had gone off the rails recently, but Sam knew that was nothing to do with her genes, it was to do with her heart and how broken it had been. What was Lena talking about, how and why would Lex create something that cheated death and chose to give it to Lena and not take it himself.

 

“Lena I don't understand? What did he mean? I thought Graves was dead how could he have visited you, you really are not making a lot of sense, I know that you are a genius but how about you cut the cryptic crap and dum it down for the students in the back huh?”

 

“He created a virus, it terminated humanity, and reanimated the subject, they require blood to survive, think Vampire, and you are going to be close, He made me a monster Sam, I am trying to control myself but, it is not easy, and I can only spend small periods out of the Lab, and only with specific people. I am using synthetic blood, but I don't trust myself with people, I don't know what I am going to do, I am a monster, I don't trust myself”

 

“Oh my god Lena, Tell me what you need? Do you need me to come to National City? Whatever you need, I can take the Jet and be with you by tomorrow. Ruby is in college but I can come, I can help with LCorp, I am not someone that can do the science stuff, but I understand the feeling, and the business. I know what it is like to not trust yourself, to know that there is something inside that you can't control, but Lena that is not you, he may have changed your biology, but he hasn't changed who you are. You're a good person. Let me help Lena please, I know things have been strained and that's on me, but you will always have me in your corner”

 

Lena was overwhelmed for a moment, Sam who she had not spoken to more than a brief word here and there was willing to drop everything and come to her, she had no idea what she had done to inspire such loyalty but she was suddenly very grateful to have someone else to rely on. She could use Sam with her even just for a few weeks until she was able to control her thirst better, until the medication gave her immunity to the sun, Lex was not going to win, she wouldn't let him.

 

“Thank you Sam, I will see you soon, tell Ruby I miss her and say hi for me.”

 

Once the phone call was over she called Jess in, the scent of Jess was tickling her throat and causing a slight burn but nothing that was unmanageable, lena raised a brow, Jess looked nervous, Lena had no idea why that would be the case, her heart was racing as she moved in, maybe she was scared to be in the office with her? Jess had shown no fear so far why would that be the issue?

 

“Jess please sit, unless being in here with me is making you uncomfortable, if that is the case I can move to the Lab”

 

“No Miss Luthor, you are not making me uncomfortable, I am sorry, I know I crossed the line with Miss Arias but I was just trying to protect you”

 

“Jess, call me Lena, and as for what you said to Sam I was impressed you are very loyal, and I need you more than ever, but I have a proposition for you, and should you choose to say no, you will still have your position as my assistant. I have come to depend on you greatly, you do a lot of the work that would be on a normal CEO’s desk and I have always been grateful for your diligence. You have worked under me for five years now, and over that time you have taken on more and more responsibilities, I am aware that you have only needed minimal instruction on new tasks, and I have leaned on you more than a CEO would normally on an assistant. As such I want to promote you, I am creating a new position, COO, Chief Operating Officer, you will be directly under me along with Sam as the CFO who will be heading back to National City for a short period. It will give you executive powers, and between you most of the functions that I have will be taken care of. Until I am in a position to retake my responsibilities, at which time Sam will return to Metropolis and you will continue as the COO, allowing me to spend more time in the lab.”

 

Jess looked at her, blinking slowly as the information settled into her mind, Lena thought that she was capable of running her company with her? She was going to make her the third person in charge of LCorp. That wasn't a promotion, a promotion would be to a manager of some description, or a low level executive, this was much more than that. She was well aware of how much work she took off Lena's plate each day but the poor woman was still in her office late into the night anyway. With her and Sam helping and a new assistant they could keep the company afloat, but who could they trust with the new situation? It wasn't as if there was a group of people in the secretarial pool that were fit for the position. No Lena needed someone that she could trust to be her assistant, Jess was that person, she knew that Lena needed her in that position why would she be suggesting that she do something else. She wasn't planning on going back to using Hope was she?

 

“Lena, there are so many people that would be more qualified for a position like that, and you need a competent assistant now more than ever, there isn't anyone on staff that would be able to do my job here, not that I would trust given what is happening with you at the moment. I think I am needed where I am at the moment, and maybe if I was to be promoted at some point I am not qualified for that position. Will Sam need an assistant? Shall I set her up in your office like before or one of the other offices on this floor?”

 

“My thought was that you would take my office, we would get you an assistant to manage your needs, one to answer the phone for me, if the call is deemed important it would be passed to you, you would have a direct line to me, Sam will be fine in the other corner office and yes she will need an assistant. As for qualifications, essentially you could consider the last five years an intense internship, trust me there is no one more qualified than you to do this job, you will learn the rest of what you need from Sam and myself. You are right at this current time there is no one that I trust to be close to me but you. As I said you can turn it down, and if you choose to do so, you will remain as my assistant, Sam will be CFO and have an assistant. However her reassignment is temporary, she still has Ruby to take care of. I will have the offer and contract drawn up, and you can think about it. Now I really need to go down to the Lab and get some sleep. The sun reaches its peak and my head feels like it's ready to drop, it’s most frustrating. I believe in you Jess, think it over ok.”

 

Lena called down to HR after Jess left to call up a package similar to the offer she gave Sam, with the new title and responsibilities, to be ready for pick up by Jess at the end of the day and retreated to the Lab.

12. Help needed

Summary for the Chapter:

Lena and Kara talk, they enlist Eliza and Alex gets suspicious.

Eliza
Maybe it was maternal instincts, that she somehow knew that she would need it, maybe the pie itself called one of her wayward daughters back to her. Either way whatever was troubling Kara would be better with pie, Kara always said that life was better with her pecan pie. She took up her seat next to Kara and placed the pie, before looking at her daughter and smiling softly.

Alex
As Harris bent to collect it using her superspeed Kara took something, Alex wouldn't have known except for the slight shift in her cape that she had even moved. So it was true to anyone who did not know Kara she could indeed be sneaky. Alex moved from her office and headed to the corridor where she ran into Kara.

******** Lena’s Lab*******

With Sam’s arrival things smoothed out, Jess wasn't willing to take the position permanently at this point but Lena knew that she could prove to her that she deserved the trust and the position, that she had worked hard for it. Between the three of them the company was running smoothly, it had been a week and Lena’s resolve was getting stronger every day, she was able to attend meetings and resist the sun's call on her mind, she ensured that she maintained an above average intake of blood, using the jelly when faced with many people. The one thing that she couldn't get passed was Kara, it pained her that she was unable to be with Kara. After the night in the cube where Kara had held her hand curled on the floor she couldn't deny that Kara loved her, her words were bolstered by the care and action she took to be with her. Kara was sure that the love for Lena was not dependent on her heartbeat and that she wanted to be with her regardless. It appeared that she even wanted the physical side of the potential relationship.

 

However, faced with Kara’s scent she had to put so much energy into maintaining her control of her desire to be with her that her need for blood seemed to increase and she would go into a frenzy. It put everyone else in danger so Lena took precautions. Firstly she moved the lab around so that all the main equipment she was using was inside the now extended cube. This meant that if Kara visited she didn't need to stop her work, once she was out of the building she would vent the lab and remove her scent, this would mean she was again free to walk about and interact with Sam and Jess. Lena’s office was well ventilated and Kara only entered the building through the balcony. Jess ensured that the blood was fully stocked and that there was enough to keep her well fed. She was able to hear and smell Kara before the scent took hold and would activate the cube.

 

Things were working well so far, however Lena felt that the amount of effort that it took to control herself seemed to affect her ability to solve the current issue. She was still unable to recreate the virus, she couldn't understand how the formula they had on file was not reproducing the virus. The latest attempt was the third failure this week, she couldn't help but wonder if the virus had somehow reduced her intelligence, that some damage was done to her mind. She threw the tablet across the lab, the results were still the same as a failure, it collided with the wall and shattered, the enhanced strength was a lot to get used to. Lena could hear the elevator open and someone step in, she listened to the heart, it was still not possible for her to distinguish between Sam and Jess, although she was able to hear the difference with Kara, her heart was slower than others, and that was the heart in the elevator at the moment, then the scent drifted down through the shaft and Lena activated the cube. She was standing leaning against the desk, both hands curls around the edge, knuckles white and the metal moaning at the stress.

 

“Hey Kara, I wasn't expecting you is everything alright?”

 

“Jess called and said that the most recent test was not quite what you wanted, she thought that a visit may cheer you up a little, I know that me being here is hard, but we both seem more content when we have time together, even if it is through a lead cube. I know that you are struggling to recreate it from your brothers notes, but he wouldn't have everything out in the open. Maybe you can compare the virus in your own blood to that you have created to see what the difference is? I am sure that Lex would have left out something crucial after all he was paranoid, especially towards the end.”

 

“I know that he would have done something like that, although I am very accomplished with some chemical and genetic interventions. I am not a bio-engineer, not like the insane doctor that created this in the first place. Give me a computer, or a mechanism to fix, a problem to solve or something to build. I am able to do that in my sleep but this, the finer points of genetic engineering concepts take me longer, and I want to be able to get this fixed before it is too late. I don't want to be responsible for any more deaths, I just can't walk down that path again.”

 

Kara moved to the opening in the cube and fixed her eyes on Lena, she wouldn't allow her in she knew that now, after a week of trying Lena’s resolve was solid. She watched as a beautiful smile graced lenas face, the way her shoulder dropped and she relaxed seeing what Kara had in mind, her eyes shone, the hint of red picking up the light, Kara couldn't help but wonder if that would have been how Lena’s eyes reacted under the light of Rao, that ring of red that replaced some of the very light grey in her eye. The grey that would have reflected Rao on her face. Lena raised her brow and Kara felt the way her heart skipped lightly, the thing with Lena being able to hear her heart now was that she would be able to hear the effect that subtle movement of her brow affected her. She waited for Lena to sit by the vent and moved her hand in, rubbing over her wrist as she held her hand.

 

“You know i have faith in you Lena, and there is nothing that you can't do given enough time, your brain is a wondrous thing and I love watching you work. I do know a bio-engeneer that could help, and no I don't mean Alex, I know that your worried about the DEO but she is not the only Danvers woman that had that talent, Eliza is much more accomplished than Alex in general, she was the one that treated Medusa, even with Alex’s additional expertise in Alien biology, it was Eliza that turned the tables on the virus. You can trust her, she won't say anything until you're ready. Please Lena think about it.”

 

“Time is something that I am not sure that I have in Kara, my fear is that even if I can reverse this, the damage over time will be insurmountable, that is why I am so frustrated by my failure. Do you really think that Eliza would be willing to help me? To keep this from Alex for now?”

 

“Lena, Eliza is a very special woman, she has so much love, you have seen what she is like, you have been on the end of one of her mother talks, she took in a thirteen year old alien, and it cost her her husband, but she continued to love me. Trust me she loves you too, you wouldn't have gotten the “you deserve love, you deserve happiness” talk if she didn't love you, plus you have something else on your side, I love you and you make me happy which is all she wants, if she can help she will, I promise.”

 

Lena closed her eyes and let the calm of Kara’s thumb stroking on her skin wash over her, she was surrounded by the scent of Kara now too, although that was less calming, the taste of Kara on her tongue was starting to awaken the need in her again, her temperature increased and her core throbbed. It was a familiar feeling as familiar as the burn in her throat, she wanted Kara on a fundamental level, biological, chemical. It could easily override the desire and love she felt for her before all of this started and she didn't want that. She wanted Kara to feel her love not her desperation, she wanted to be her again before they went any further. She thought about Kara’s words Eliza, was a very accomplished scientist, far better with genealogy and viruses than Lena was, she would be invaluable in the quest for the virus and the cure. If they could get to the cure quicker then maybe she would be able to cure herself, what greater gift could she give Kara than the return of her heart beat?

 

“Ok Kara, we will ask your mother to come and help, but we need to keep it from Alex for now, she has a duty to the DEO, it isn't just aliens that they monitor, but all supernatural and metal human activities. You know this, and although I am not a mythical vampire or a meta human there is definitely a difference in me that could easily be considered a threat, not just to the human population but the alien too. What if I were to consume the blood of an alien? Did taking in the essence of aliens and meta’s increase the strength of that parasite that you fought? Imagine what I could become with the powers you and others possess. What if I turned you, or others? Create an army of the undead, I am sure that was what my brother intended.”

 

“I have said it before Lena, and I will keep saying it until you hear me and believe it for yourself, you are not your family, you are not your mother or your brother, you are Lena, you have the biggest heart and the most beautiful soul, the Luthor name doesn't deserve you, it never has. Whatever Lex had planned for this virus, for you, for his revenge, we will beat him together as we always have, we are stronger together Lena. I will go to Midvale to see Eliza and ask for her help, she will come here, she will help I know she will.”

 

“Thank you Kara, I don't want to be like this, a monster……. No don’t please, it is how I feel, I have to drink blood, I want nothing more than to attack you nearly all of the time, and you can stop that look, as much as you say you would be happy, I love you too much to take you like that, I want it to be me, all of me not some crazy out of control demon that wants to ravage you at all costs. I should warn you I can feel starting already, being this close is hard Kara, I will need to feed again and you should go to see your mother, the sooner we start the sooner we can fix this right.”

 

Kara couldn't help the way that her mind and body caught on to the inflection of Lena’s voice, the look on her face and the words that she spoke, her heart swelled at the words of love, lust and desire, it fanned Kara’s own and she tried to control it she knew that the scent of her arousal would cause Lena more difficulties. She was more happy to stay than to head off to Midvale but Lena told her that she could already feel the need and the desire to feed again so soon she decided to leave. She pulled her hand through the opening to kiss it tenderly before releasing her, standing again she said her goodbyes and headed back out of the lab she would go to Eliza now, there was after all not time like the present.

 

*******Midvale*******

 

Eliza sat in her kitchen, looking over her latest results from the lab, a cup of steaming tea in her hands, she was coming to the end of her latest project and the projections were good. There was no reason to think that these projections would fare any less effectively with human trials, although there was still some time before the FDA would approve them, it was the problem with science the number of times the results needed to be repeated not only by the originator but others. The results needed to be replicated by other labs to prove their effectiveness before human trials would be allowed. As if she would falsify the results and risk other people's lives to forward her own theories. She looked up to the window as a familiar sound broke her from her thoughts, there was no mistaking the sound of soft leather boots landing on the porch. She stood from the table and made her way to the door opening it wide and smiling at the sight of Kara heading towards her, she opened her arms to embrace her although she noticed two things as she came closer. One that she seemed lighter, her shoulders not so tight a spring in her step as she moved forward. Two that there was a small crinkle between her brows, which indicated trouble. How was it possible for both to be true?

 

“Kara sweetheart, what do I owe for the pleasure of your visit, and what is going on, you seem happy but worried is everything alright darling?”

 

“Hey Eliza, it is so nice to see you, and you're right I am happy, I finally told Lena how I feel, and she loves me back, the reason for this visit and it will take some explaining. Do you have time?”

 

“For you always, I am happy for you both, come on in, I must have sensed your arrival, I made pie, let's sit in the kitchen and we can talk and eat.”

 

Eliza threw her arm around Kara’s shoulders and guided her into the kitchen sitting her down and pouring her some tea before heading to the fridge and taking out the pie, chocolate pecan Kara’s favourite, although there had been no news from National city, she had felt the urge to bake yesterday and chocolate pecan was the only thing that she could focus on. Maybe it was maternal instincts, that she somehow knew that she would need it, maybe the pie itself called one of her wayward daughters back to her. Either way whatever was troubling Kara would be better with pie, Kara always said that life was better with her pecan pie. She took up her seat next to Kara and placed the pie, before looking at her daughter and smiling softly.

 

“So tell me what is happening? Is something wrong with Alex, or maybe Nia?”

 

“No Eliza, Alex is fine, so is Nia, it's Lena that needs help. Lex created a virus that she has been infected by, and she is trying to work on recreating it from the research she found but he has left something out of the research and she is having difficulty working it out. She is brilliant and I am sure given time she would work it all out but she doesn't think that she has enough time to work it out.”

 

“Is Alex not helping? She would be able to help map the genome, then an antivirus can be worked on, don't tell me she has still not put aside the issues with Lena? Why didn't you call me, I can talk to her.”

 

“No Eliza, you don't understand, um, Lena has been infected with the virus……...Alex doesn't know, Lena wants to keep it that way for now………. you see the effects of the virus isn't making Lena sick, it has made her, um, undead, or that's what we are calling it, a…….. a vampire of sorts. Rao it's hard, um…. it's hard to explain but Lena is no longer living in the literal sense of the word, but is walking around and thinking talking everything as normal….. And then it isn't, not really……. Um, but at the same time, she is dead, her heart doesn't beat and she needs to drink blood to remain herself, she has, well um, synthetic blood she isn't hurting anyone…..she has, enhancements too…. She is almost as strong as me...fast too….. She can smell as good as me, and my scent is an issue…... Rao when I try to explain it, she sounds dangerous and well it sounds crazy right….but by Rao its the truth, she is fighting to stay who she is and is worried the DEO will take her and she won't be able to keep herself in control. There are others, and Alex will go after them….. Which needs to be done, but…..LenaI love her and she needs to be saved and so I was hoping that you could help us?”

 

That was a lot to take in, Eliza took Kara’s hand and looked into her face, she completely believed what she was saying, she had not heard such a ramble from her daughter in a while, she wasn't wrong, Alex would want to take Lena to the DEO, the government would want to harness this power soldiers that couldn't be killed as they were already dead, that would be an insurmountable obstacle in war. A person that was as strong and fast as Kara, that was dead and couldn't die. She could understand Lena’s reticence about involving Alex, but surely Kara believed that Alex wouldn't hurt her, and hurting Lena would hurt Kara. She was intrigued by this virus too, something that could reanimate dead cells and allow the person to continue as they were with additional abilities. She didn't want to tell Kara that there was little that could be done to reverse death, and whatever was reanimating Lena once it was done would likely return her to a state of death. Although she couldn't believe that Lena would not have realised this, and on some level Kara must too, she was a scientist from an advanced race, even though her knowledge was cut short at thirteen she would know this much.

 

“Kara, sweetheart, of course I will come and help Lena, I want to look at the science behind what is happening, I am sure that Lena has already made progress. Would you be able to get a kryptonite canular from the DEO? Your ability to heal could help fight the virus, your white blood cells have very little use against human viruses in general as you have not had contact with them, but the essence of your abilities could be of use. Your immune system is amazing; it just never had the chance to combate earth illnesses.”

 

“I can try but I don't want to make Alex suspicious, I won't betray Lena, and risk her being taken by the government, she doesn't want to be like her family and they will make her a weapon, I know that Alex wouldn't hurt her, hurt me, but she isn't the only person in the DEO.”

 

Eliza couldn't help but wonder if there was a way for Kara to give the necessary regeneration to Lena, she wouldn't want to produce a way to take or copy Kara’s abilities, to use her DNA to create more like her, not everyone had her kind heart, but if there was a way to get Kara’s healing and regeneration to work on Lena, to heal the body before the virus was completely removed there may be a chance that destroying the virus, that if the virus was keeping her alive to a point as Kara suggested that she would be able to keep her alive once it was gone. She just wanted her daughters to be happy, that's all that any mother wants but curing the virus could kill Lena and she didn't want to be responsible for taking anything else from Kara, her precious daughter had lost so much already. Finishing her tea she stood from the table to prepare for a visit to National City. Kara following her as she started to pack.

 

“Are you taking me or am I making my own way? I think there is a good chance either way that Alex will know that I am around, we will need to tell her something, although that is probably best left to me, you have never been good at lying Kara, not that I minded as a child, it was easier to know the truth, but if Lena doesn't want Alex involved, right now it would be helpful.”

 

“Hey I can keep a secret, I managed to get Alex off our backs once, and I can be sneaky too, why does everyone think this of me?”

 

“Oh I don't know sweetheart, maybe because your entire body rejects the lies, causing the crinkle and stuttering, inability to form eye contact or control your blushing? Your entire body rejects the lies Kara, anyone that knows you as well as Alex and I will see through it, tell me how did you throw her off before?”

 

“I um, well…. I brought up Mon-El.”

 

Eliza shook her head lightly, that wasn't something that she would expect from Kara, although Alex possibly, she managed to use Alex’s guilt against her, and Eliza had a part in that too, it was her after all that was so hard on Alex, getting her to protect Kara at all costs, she gave her this need, this guilt, this feeling that she wasn't good enough, she regretted it immensely, and although she had apologised she could see that there was some of that pain left in her daughter if Kara had been able to manipulate the situation using it. Things were better with Alex than they had been in a long time but she couldn't feel that there was still much to make up for. At least Kara looked as though she felt guilty for it, but that wasn't something that she wanted her doing again, if she needed to have a way to get Alex to back down then they should have another way to do it, one that didn't hurt either of her daughters.

 

“Kara, please don't do that to Alex again. It will only hurt her and you in the long run, you both deserve to have your secrets and things that you keep to yourselves, but that causes damage to, maybe you can have an agreement that when it is important you can be honest, say that you can't explain at the moment but that you need her to understand, that you will tell her when the time is right, I know she is protective, overly some times, and that is my fault, so is the weakness you used against her, but you are sisters, and you shouldn't do that to one another. Promise me that you will find another way in future.”

 

“I promise, I am sorry Eliza, but I couldn't think of another way, the DEO were investigating and if they found the truth it would have been something that I couldn't stop, I needed to protect Lena.”

 

“I understand, you love her, and that is a beautiful thing, but not at the expense of your sister, you are a brilliant woman, and a kind heart, I know you will find another way.”

 

Eliza finished packing up and made a few phone calls before she was ready to leave, they decided that if Alex was to know she was there then she would stay at her normal hotel. They had decided that they would use Eliza’s current research as a reason for the visit and that Lena would be trying to recreate the findings to satisfy the FDA, it gave Eliza a reason to be at LCorp and a reason to be in National city. They needed to ensure that Alex stayed away from LCorp, even with Eliza there Kara didn't want to do anything to put Lena under any more stress. Kara took Eliza to the hotel to settle in before heading off for the DEO.

 

*******DEO******

 

Alex stood in her office, she was going over some basic paperwork, not something that she relished about her new position but it needed to be done. The good thing about her office was that she could view the DEO and no one knew who was watching. On a day like today she was most glad of this view, she watched as Kara landed and strolled into the command centre, she was swinging her arms and rocking on her feet, to casual, to care free, once again she was trying to hard again, it sparked her attention and she watched over the top of her paperwork. Kara chatted and laughed with Brainy, looked at the screens as he spoke, although tilting her head in the way that told Alex, Kara was listening to something other than the conversation, Alex calmed her breath and focused on keeping her heart rate steady. When Kara glanced up she made no attempt to acknowledge her just turned the page to convey that she was deep in thought. She observed the small curl of Kara’s lips and the glance to the med bay and lab. Kara was definitely trying to accomplish something that she didn't want Alex to know, that sparked her attention even more than the exaggerated movements.

 

Alex could hear Kara’s laugh, it was forces and loud and too controlled, she was putting on a show, chatting to agents as they walked back and for, then she moved towards the med bay to talk to Vasquez, Susan seemed to be talking to her, Alex could only imagine what she was saying based on the redness that was showing on her sisters face. Susan laughed and Kara bit her lip, that meant one thing only it was about Lena. Alex turned the page and sat on the edge of the desk casually allowing her peripheral vision to extend to Kara. She watched as Kara seemed to notice the change and then look to the med bay, Alex knew well enough to know the tilt of her head was an x-ray scan of the bay, what was it that Kara was looking for, and why couldn't she just come to Alex about it?
