Lena was overwhelmed with the emotion that had crept in from Alex’s words rolling around in her head, clearly they had all accepted her, even like this.

 

Too emotional to work, Lena looked at her phone and thought about calling Kara back. She knew that she would be there in seconds, wrapping her up in her arms. But that was the opposite of what she needed, everything always seemed perfect when Kara was there and she knew that wasn't the truth, the problems were still there, the world kept spinning and decisions still needed to be made. This would be the kind of night that she would drain a bottle of her vintage scotch, but she wasn't sure that she could even get drunk now, or how much she would need to achieve the black out drunken state that she would opt for on a night like this. She wanted to shove everything into a box, to squash it down and hide it in the deep recesses of her mind. When she started there was supposed to be time to go back and work through it but now she knew that was not the case. She wouldn't work through anything unless it exploded in her face. Kelly had cautioned her on this behaviour too so she decided to allow the uncomfortable emotions and the thoughts to circle in her mind until she fell asleep, tear stained and exhausted.

 

The next morning Alex had sent her the information that she had asked for, along with a brief message.

 

Lena Luthor from Alex Danvers

Please find attached the files, the second is 80% corrupted which maybe you can fix although Brainy was unable to, the others seem to have information but nothing that actually holds the answers, I have run every known cypher, description and analysis programme to uncover hidden data, like with the photo, unfortunately there was nothing new to find.

I hope that there is something in your amazing brain that helps, your knowledge of the author may prove helpful in this case.

Lena I know you asked for time but if you need to talk you can call me, or Kelly.

 

Alex.

 

She could feel the emotion welling up again and decided that she needed to focus on work, she couldn't lose more time crying into her blanket. One night was self indulgent enough, and now she needed to work out what the hell her mother had done to reanimate a heart. She opened the files and started to go through them. She read them looking for anything hidden, any message that was there she would understand. For the most part it was written like a theoretical paper with no substance. Not Lillian's style at all, as much as she wanted people to look up to her and admire her and her process and power, she much more enjoyed the way she could write things in a way that was both demeaning to the audience as well as completely ingratiating her own intelligence. This was more like a puff piece you would find in a pop psych publication, watered down and fluffy in a way that Lillian was never. Lena couldn't help but think that this was written by someone else and saved in the file for distraction. There was nothing about it that read like Lillian at all, the second one was much the same, it wasn't something that she could reconcile with her understanding of Lillian, it was creating a level of cognitive dissonance in her that was bordering on torturous. Even in this Lillian was taunting her, the one thing that could help her was obscured. Her only thought was that the corrupted file must have been the real one and that the corruption was in fact something that she used to protect the real information.

 

Based on her knowledge of the DEO training and the way that Brainy worked she could discount most of the normal algorithms, and her knowledge of Lillian told her it wouldn't be something as simple. She started the description that she created to encrypt Luthor corp information, it was a long shot but she needed somewhere to start, and while it was running she was able to get her supplement. She returned with her warmed synthetic slush and sat at the desk, working on the different files, she had a number of options running, but nothing was getting anywhere. She started looking at other options, using greek myth, russian theology, classics references, chess moves, family information there was nothing, no change to the corrupted parts. Lena looked up, her eyes stinging and blurry to see the sky had darkened, she had no idea how long she had been working on this but she couldn't stare at the screen any more. She decided to take a break, print the complete documents and while they were printing take a shower. Anything that wasn't staring at the screen.

 

In the shower she found herself dwelling on thoughts of Kara, once again the desire to call her crept up and she knew that this would be something that would continue, it was like this before to a degree but since the virus it was more potent, more visceral, she could feel her core reacting to the thought of her, she couldn't even smell her, or see her and she was becoming aroused. As the point of this isolation was to make a decision that was based on what she wanted not what other people thought she knew that she needed this time but it was a lot harder than she thought that it would be, it was almost impossible to imagine a life without her at this point, but that wasn't her option at the moment, to live like this, or to try and cure herself, which would potentially lose her physical relationship with Kara and leave Kara alone one day. With the Luthor life expectancy that wouldn't be a long time from now, Lionel was in his fifties, Lex his forties and Lena well she was already getting close to thirty. The oldest living Luthor was Lillian but she was as slippery as a snake and only a Luthor by name. She was pinning her hopes on this information from the CADMUS hall of horrors.

 

Once finished she moved back into the room and picked up the papers placing them on the table and sitting down. She decided to get herself a glass of red and sat drinking it, while relaxing a little mulling over the possibilities a little more, coming up with no more options or conclusions than in the shower. She placed the glass gown on the table and stopped looking through the base of the glass. The corner of the page was magnified by the glass and she could see a small marking, she laughed a little and jumped up grabbing the glass and papers heading into the dinning room. Scattering the papers over the table she then headed to the home office for a magnifying glass, if it had not been for her enhanced vision she wouldn't have noticed the markings even with the wine glass. Hurrying back into the dining room she took a note pad and started mapping the markings, it took a while to get the system that Lillian had used, it wasn't a simple overlay, of course not it was a three dimensional shape. Lena laughed even though her hidden messages were pretentious, the first paper 20 pages long was an Icosahedron, the markings creating a complex folding of the papers into the three dimensional structure, there was nothing here that made any more sense but she knew she was onto something. The second a dodecahedron using the 12 pages.

 

She looked at them sitting on her table, the complex shapes didn't give a clue as to what she was looking she looked at each of the faces and realised that it was a program within the writing, it was the way that the pages connected, she wasn't sure what made her think of it but it was a map, the connections, the cross overs, it looked like the old geometric primitive, from old computer systems. Taking note of the positioning she went to the laptop and started rearranging the pages into the formation and unlocked the programme, it caused a new file to be embedded within the folder, the second one was completed the same. She let out a small cry of triumph and clicked the first file. To find another layer of security, this was more complicated than she thought. The first puzzle is always the easiest in the Luthor house, lull them into a false sense of security, make them over confident and then knock them down to size. Lillian was particularly good at this. She looked at the sequence of numbers and pinched the bridge of her nose. She jotted down the sequence, and opened the other file, again to be met with a sequence of words. She jotted them down too and decided to go back to the corrupted file and print out the pages that were not corrupted.

 

The new pages had a similar pattern of markings that she could see, the issue was the full document was 43 pages and there were only 9 accessible. She knew that this was going to be impossible without the rest of the pages, although she had no idea what kind of complex shape this would be, with 43 pages, it wouldn't be enough to create a sphere, way too much for any other geometric shape. Not that this made very much difference at the moment as she still had not thought of a way to fix the programme, she was starting to believe it was actual corruption not a protection. If Brainy was not able to salvage it she would need a copy of the original file, and that wouldn't be easy to get, the CADMUS servers were damaged during the take down and she wasn't sure if they were backed up the only way to know would be to contact Lillian.

 

Sat back on the couch Lena looked at her notes and tried to work on the sequencing, the numbers made little sense, neither did the words however they seemed familiar, when rearranging them they formed part of a quote, from Henry the V, of course it would, the invasion of france by a man that felt he was entitled to the throne.

 

“Every subject’s duty is the king’s, but every subject’s soul is his own. We few, we happy few, we band of brothers. Shall be my brother. There is occasions and causes why and wherefore in all things. A good heart, Kate, is the sun and the moon— or rather the sun and not the moon, for it shines bright and never changes, but keeps his course truly.”

 

Looking back at the numbers they instantly made sense they were the numbers for the date of the two estranged brothers birthdays, Lena sighed, few people knew even what family Lillan belonged let alone that she had two brothers, that meant that she could unpack the programmes, she imputed the information and opened the files to see two very technical diagrammes, they would make two pieces of technology, one to receive signals and regulate the heart the other to send signals, she wasn't sure why there were two, she looked into the schematics and realised that Lillian was working on the autonomic functions of the brain. She had not even considered that to be a factor, the medulla oblongata normally controls the breathing and the heart rate, the other autonomic functions, if that was damaged there would not be a way to regulate the system. Clearly the people that Lillian had worked on had issues with their brains too. She looked more closely and there was a system inside the schematics that would cause a complete system overload with a tone. Lena raised her brow and thought back to the gang with alien weapons. They had all mysteriously died with no cause. She wouldn't be incorporating that but the idea gave her a lot to think about. Firstly she would need help from Eliza.

 

*****Lena’s Lab******

 

The next morning Lena had another fMRI this time however of her brain mapping the functions of her brain, Eliza didn't ask questions, did not prompt for small talk, Lena appreciated that she was just there to help facilitate and didn't push her to talk about anything, sure she could see that Eliza was curious and that she wanted to talk. Once the scan was complete and the results sent to Lena she got dressed and came into the lab.

 

“Thank you for your help today Eliza, I have made some progress with the CADMUS information although there appears to be a corrupted file that means I am missing something but I think that I have something that may be useful. I haven't made any decisions yet but I will be back at work tomorrow, so I will call Kara later to let her know.”

 

“That is not a problem Lena, as I said I will help any way that I can, and I am sure that Kara will be ecstatic to hear that. You know that Teddy already looks like its years old, not brand new, you may want to take a deep breath when she hugs you next you may get a full kryptonian power hug, even Kal cringes.”

 

Lena laughed and accepted a small hug before leaving to head back to the penthouse, picking up the bag she needed before heading to the elevator. Once back she checked the results and her brain was functional but the Medulla oblongata was not showing as brightly as it should. She could make a modified version of both devices, she hoped that with her modifications that they would work and whatever was missing would not be needed.

 

*****Lena’s Penthouse******

 

Lena worked into the evening creating the devices and then went back over the information for the virus, the termination program was contained in a implan within the head, it had Lillian all over it, and she couldn't help but wonder if Lillian knew more about this project than she thought, if she was creating the parts maybe they had a cure, she wondered if Lex had at some point been infected, although there was no way he would give up this power. It was clear there was more information but it was not contained in the files, Lena started to make a list of all the people connected in the project. She needed information and that was one thing her money was good for, she would be able to pay for answers. She worked diligently through the list.

 

Lena picked up her phone and dialed the number not sure if what she was doing was right or not but she wanted to see what would happen if she reached out at all. The phone rang a couple of times before the call connected, she had a brief conversation and then the call was terminated. It had been mostly as she expected but reaching out to anyone that still believed in the Luthor corp regim was largely pointless. She would not get any answers this way, the list was now all but crossed off, she sighed and then there was a notification someone was accessing the old servers. Lena watched as whoever was going through accessed a number of files including the regeneration project and Lex’s video to her. She could be in trouble if they wanted the project in the dark. Someone could be coming after her very soon. Lena closed her laptop she would need to prepare, although she was less vulnerable now, they knew about the project, would know what she was and just how to kill her. She thought about her new family and sighed, she would need to tell them what happened and there would be additional concerns for LCorp and all of them. She called Kara.

 

“Hello darling, I have missed you.”

 

“Lena, it's so good to hear your voice. Are you ready to see me now? I can be there in like two seconds, I mean like literally.”

 

“Not just yet can you gather everyone and meet me in the lab in the morning, we need to talk, and then after maybe a date night?”

 

“Um ok, everyone that was there last time? I can do that? What time?”

 

“Let's say eight, get an early start?”

 

“OK, I can't wait to see you, I have missed you Lena.”

 

“I love you Kara, see you soon.”

 

“I love you, Lena”

 

Lena smiled and hung up the call. She moved to the kitchen and prepared herself dinner, if you can call a unit of synthetic blood that, she made her way into the lounge and drank her meal, going through the papers once again, there was nothing new in them of course but she still looked, something anything, she just wanted there to be a hint, a bread crumb something. Eventually Lena decided to go to bed. There was no point in continuing this, she was just frustrating herself. There were still bits missing from every answer and Lex had cleaned up so well there was noone left to find. Everyone that worked on the project bar the two she had spoken to were dead, and that meant just another nail in her coffin. She was woken by a call about an hour later, and to her surprise the outcome was better than she could have dreamed of.

The group were collected as Lena had requested, three days ago they had all been here to try to get Lena to do what was best for her, to live, they each had different agendas and loyalties, but in the last three days they had come to an understanding. Whatever choice they would help, and they would be there for Lena until she was as she wanted to be or was no longer alive. Then they would all be there for Kara, each of the group was invested in doing the best they could, Alex had spent three days studying the CADMUS information, Eliza had tried another two versions of the cure with no greater success. They had all taken turns entertaining Kara who had stayed away from Lena. Kara was the only one not seated; her nervous and excited energy seemed to be flooding the lab as she bounced on her feet and fiddled with her sleeves. Then the sound they were all waiting for the elevator, Kara was practically vibrating. As the chime sounded the arrival there was a brief gasp from Kara, then the group heads all moved to face the elevator. The doors opened and there was the ever prominent click clack of Lena’s heels in stereo as she stepped out of the elevator flanked by Lillian Luthor. Stopping Lillian looked up with a sardonic smile and a raised brow. "what not pleased to see me"

 

To be continued on Part 2 coming soon to AO3

26. Sneak peak

Summary for the Chapter:

Spoiler alert for thoughs that can't wait, beginning of the new arch.....

************ present day suit in hotel outside of National city*********

 

Lillian Luthor was not one for second guessing, for reflecting on decisions or doubting her resolve. She knew her path, her purpose and what needed to be done to achieve her goals. She was not accustomed to watching either, sure she observed all things, the government, medical professionals, aliens, the general socio economic climate. But she was never a passive watcher, oh far from it, she skilfully and wilfully manipulated the options, the decisions, the path that people were taking. Moving them forward into the positions that she needed them to be to get what she needed. She was Athena, manoeuvring the armies into position, Aries firing up the armies, Loki confusing the masses, she was Zeus ruling all, the master strategist, the puppet master, defining the outcomes, it had worked until Medusa. It was a surprise to her that the daughter she never asked for was the one to out manoeuvre her. A master in strategy herself she had been able to see the moves, the use of Henshaw in gathering the final piece had tipped her hand, the intrusion of the kryptonian had sparked the moment that Lena connected the dots and unravelled the strategy she had been working on.

 

It had surprised her to a point, she knew that Lena was brilliant, that she was wilful and that she didn't believe in the cause, not like her beloved Lex did. She was corrupted by the alien fascination and the desire to help on a small scale not eliminate the problems in grand ways. She thought that she had done enough over the years to curb her resolve but it appears that it had not been the case. She was both extremely disappointed, impressed and annoyed at the revelations of Medusa. It was the point that some level of doubt crept into Lillian's initial thoughts not only about Lena but about the choice of having Lex as her successor, not that she allowed them to manifest at that point, after all she needed to get things done, and being in prison gave her the ability to implement another plan of action. However that seed that tiny glimmer of doubt was now present again.

 

Sitting in the less than satisfactory hotel suit, eating a over cooked steak that was most definitely not rib eye, and sipping a glass of red, the vastly inflated priced bottle that stated it was indeed what she had asked for, the Domaine de la Romanee-Conti Romanee-Conti 1990 had clearly long since been consumed and they had replaced it with a mediocre and sub standard generic red. As if she couldn't tell the difference, as if her pallet was not augmented to someone of discerning tastes, did they not realise that Lillian Luthor the matriarch of the Luthor family that could buy this mediocre excuse for a hotel a million times over would not realise the difference. She pushed the plate away and stood, glancing at the remnants of a very unsatisfactory meal and moved to the couch, she needed to think about what was happening. See what the endgame was becoming so that she could understand if her presence was indeed needed.
